00459: def save_transport_ready(state: Mapping[str, Any], path: str | Path) -> Path:
00460:     """Validate and save a portable CPU-tensor transport artifact."""
00461: 
00462:     validate_transport_ready(state)
00463:     path = Path(path)

00001: """Minimal RealWonder Wan VAE-only codec without text or diffusion models."""

00004: 
00005: import importlib.util

00008: 
00009: import torch
00010: import torch.nn as nn
00011: 
00012: 
00013: # Importing ``wan.modules.vae`` normally executes ``wan/__init__.py``, which
00014: # eagerly imports the diffusion model and requires unrelated packages. Loading
00015: # this standalone module file preserves RealWonder's exact VAE implementation
00016: # while keeping the VAE-only stage independent of diffusers, PEFT, T5, and CLIP.
00017: _VAE_SOURCE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "wan" / "modules" / "vae.py"
00018: _VAE_SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
00019:     "deform_transport_realwonder_wan_vae", _VAE_SOURCE
00020: )
00021: if _VAE_SPEC is None or _VAE_SPEC.loader is None:
00022:     raise ImportError(f"cannot load RealWonder Wan VAE source from {_VAE_SOURCE}")
00023: _VAE_MODULE = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_VAE_SPEC)
00024: sys.modules[_VAE_SPEC.name] = _VAE_MODULE
00025: _VAE_SPEC.loader.exec_module(_VAE_MODULE)
00026: _video_vae = _VAE_MODULE._video_vae

00069: 
00070:     Wan encodes frame zero alone, then consecutive four-frame chunks. For 21
00071:     pixel frames this produces six latent slots aligned to `[0,4,8,12,16,20]`.
00072:     """
00073: 
00074:     if pixel_frame_count < 1:

00080: 
00081: class RealWonderWanVAECodec(nn.Module):
00082:     """The exact 16-channel Wan2.1 VAE and normalization used by RealWonder."""
00083: 
00084:     def __init__(
00085:         self,
00086:         checkpoint_path: str | Path,
00087:         *,
00088:         device: torch.device | str,
00089:         dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16,
00090:     ) -> None:
00091:         super().__init__()
00092:         self.checkpoint_path = Path(checkpoint_path)
00093:         if not self.checkpoint_path.is_file():
00094:             raise FileNotFoundError(self.checkpoint_path)
00095:         self.compute_device = torch.device(device)
00096:         self.compute_dtype = dtype
00097:         self.model = _video_vae(
00098:             pretrained_path=str(self.checkpoint_path), z_dim=16, device="cpu"
00099:         ).eval().requires_grad_(False)
00100:         self.model.to(device=self.compute_device, dtype=self.compute_dtype)
00101:         self.register_buffer(
00102:             "latent_mean", torch.tensor(WAN_VAE_MEAN, dtype=torch.float32)
00103:         )
00104:         self.register_buffer(
00105:             "latent_inverse_std",
00106:             1.0 / torch.tensor(WAN_VAE_STD, dtype=torch.float32),
00107:         )
00108: 
00109:     def _scale(self, dtype: torch.dtype) -> list[torch.Tensor]:
00110:         return [
00111:             self.latent_mean.to(device=self.compute_device, dtype=dtype),
00112:             self.latent_inverse_std.to(device=self.compute_device, dtype=dtype),
00113:         ]
00114: 
00115:     @torch.inference_mode()
00116:     def encode_pixels(self, pixels: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
00117:         """Encode `[B,C,T,H,W]` pixels in `[-1,1]` to `[B,Tz,16,h,w]`."""

--- LINES 162-186 ---
00162:         })
00163: 
00164: 
00165: def generation_loop(prompt):
00166:     """Main generation loop with 3-stage streaming pipeline.
00167: 
00168:     Stage 1 (thread): Simulation — produces RGB frames + optical flows per block
00169:     Stage 2 (thread): Noise warping — warps noise using optical flow (lightweight)
00170:     Stage 3 (main):   VAE encoding + mask building + diffusion denoising + streaming
00171: 
00172:     Each stage runs concurrently: while VGen denoises block N, noise warping
00173:     handles block N+1, and simulation produces block N+2. All heavy GPU work
00174:     (VAE encode + diffusion) is consolidated in Stage 3 to avoid GPU memory
00175:     contention.
00176:     """
00177:     global is_generating, stop_requested
00178:     is_generating = True
00179:     torch.set_grad_enabled(False)  # thread-local: must set in this thread too
00180: 
00181:     try:
00182:         socketio.emit("status", {"message": "Preparing video generator..."})
00183: 
00184:         # Reset noise warper before sim threads start.
00185:         noise_warper.reset()

00679:     def save_optical_flow(self, optical_flow, valid_mask, frame_id):
00680:         """Save optical flow visualization to disk (debug mode only)."""
00681:         flow_x = optical_flow[:, :, 0].cpu().numpy()
00682:         flow_y = optical_flow[:, :, 1].cpu().numpy()
00683:         valid_mask_np = valid_mask.cpu().numpy()
00684: 
00685:         angle = np.arctan2(-flow_y, flow_x)
00686:         hsv = np.zeros((optical_flow.shape[0], optical_flow.shape[1], 3), dtype=np.uint8)
00687:         hsv[..., 0] = (angle + np.pi) / (2 * np.pi) * 179
00688:         hsv[..., 1] = 255
00689:         hsv[..., 2] = 255
00690:         hsv[~valid_mask_np] = 0
00691:         flow_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(hsv, cv2.COLOR_HSV2RGB)

00947: 
00948:         flow_alpha = flow_image[0, :, :, 3]
00949:         valid_mask = flow_alpha > self.config['alpha_threshold']
00950: 
00951:         optical_flow = torch.zeros(image_size, image_size, 3, device=self.device)
00952: 
00953:         if valid_mask.sum() > 0 and max_val - min_val > 1e-4:
00954:             rendered_flow = flow_image[0, :, :, :2][valid_mask]
00955:             zero_pixels = torch.all(rendered_flow < 0.05, dim=-1)
00956:             normal_pixels = ~zero_pixels
00957: 
00958:             full_flow = torch.zeros_like(rendered_flow)
00959:             if normal_pixels.sum() > 0:
00960:                 full_flow[normal_pixels] = (
00961:                     (rendered_flow[normal_pixels] - 0.1) / 0.8 * (max_val - min_val) + min_val
00962:                 )
00963: 
00964:             optical_flow[:, :, :2][valid_mask] = full_flow
00965: 
00966:             if self.config.get('debug', False):
00967:                 meaningful_mask = valid_mask.clone()
00968:                 valid_coords = torch.where(valid_mask)
00969:                 meaningful_mask[valid_coords[0][zero_pixels], valid_coords[1][zero_pixels]] = False
00970:                 self.save_optical_flow(optical_flow, meaningful_mask, frame_id)
00971: 
00972:         return optical_flow
00973: 
00974:     @property
00975:     def num_fg_objects(self):
00976:         return len(self.fg_pcs)

00011: class CausalInferencePipeline(torch.nn.Module):
00012:     """Pipeline for causal video generation inference."""
00013: 
00014:     def __init__(self, args, device, generator=None, text_encoder=None, vae=None):
00015:         super().__init__()
00016: 
00017:         if args.i2v_flow:
00018:             self.i2v_flow = True
00019:             Wrapper = WanI2VDiffusionWrapper
00020:         else:
00021:             self.i2v_flow = False
00022:             Wrapper = WanDiffusionWrapper
00023: 
00024:         # Initialize models
00025:         self.generator = Wrapper(
00026:             **getattr(args, "model_kwargs", {}), is_causal=True) if generator is None else generator
00027:         self.text_encoder = WanTextEncoder() if text_encoder is None else text_encoder
00028:         self.vae = WanVAEWrapper() if vae is None else vae
00029: 
00030:         # Initialize causal hyperparameters
00031:         self.scheduler = self.generator.get_scheduler()
00032:         self.denoising_step_list = torch.tensor(args.denoising_step_list, dtype=torch.long)
00033:         if args.warp_denoising_step:
00034:             timesteps = torch.cat((self.scheduler.timesteps.cpu(), torch.tensor([0], dtype=torch.float32)))
00035:             self.denoising_step_list = timesteps[1000 - self.denoising_step_list]
00036:

00038:         self.frame_seq_length = 1560

--- LINES 1-53 ---
00001: """Causal inference pipeline with SDEdit support for simulation-guided video generation.
00002: 
00003: Based on vidgen/pipeline.py (CausalInferencePipeline) with SDEdit logic from
00004: causal_inference_sdedit.py. This file is kept separate to avoid modifying the
00005: cleaned vidgen module.
00006: """
00007: 
00008: from typing import List, Optional
00009: import torch
00010: 
00011: from vidgen.models import WanDiffusionWrapper, WanI2VDiffusionWrapper, WanTextEncoder, WanVAEWrapper
00012: from vidgen.memory import gpu, get_cuda_free_memory_gb, move_model_to_device_with_memory_preservation
00013: from vidgen.utils import extract_subdim
00014: 
00015: 
00016: class CausalInferencePipelineSDEdit(torch.nn.Module):
00017:     """Pipeline for causal video generation inference with SDEdit support."""
00018: 
00019:     def __init__(self, args, device, generator=None, text_encoder=None, vae=None,
00020:                  use_separate_encode_vae=False):
00021:         super().__init__()
00022: 
00023:         if args.i2v_flow:
00024:             self.i2v_flow = True
00025:             Wrapper = WanI2VDiffusionWrapper
00026:         else:
00027:             self.i2v_flow = False
00028:             Wrapper = WanDiffusionWrapper
00029: 
00030:         # Initialize models
00031:         self.generator = Wrapper(
00032:             **getattr(args, "model_kwargs", {}), is_causal=True) if generator is None else generator
00033:         self.text_encoder = WanTextEncoder() if text_encoder is None else text_encoder
00034:         self.vae = WanVAEWrapper() if vae is None else vae
00035: 
00036:         # Separate VAE for encoding sim_latents to avoid cache conflicts
00037:         # with the decoder's streaming cache on self.vae
00038:         if use_separate_encode_vae:
00039:             self.encode_vae = WanVAEWrapper()
00040:         else:
00041:             self.encode_vae = self.vae
00042: 
00043:         # Initialize causal hyperparameters
00044:         self.scheduler = self.generator.get_scheduler()
00045:         self.denoising_step_list = torch.tensor(args.denoising_step_list, dtype=torch.long)
00046: 
00047:         # Detect SDEdit mode: if the first denoising step < total scheduler steps,
00048:         # we start from a noised version of sim_latent instead of pure noise.
00049:         if self.denoising_step_list[0] < len(self.scheduler.timesteps):
00050:             self.sdedit = True
00051:         else:
00052:             self.sdedit = False
00053:

00380:     def process(self, vae, input_image, end_image, num_frames, height, width, device, torch_dtype):
00381:         image = input_image.to(device)
00382:         msk = torch.ones(1, num_frames, height // 8, width // 8, device=device)
00383:         msk[:, 1:] = 0
00384:         vae_input = torch.concat([image.transpose(0, 1),
00385:                                   torch.zeros(3, num_frames - 1, height, width).to(image.device)], dim=1)
00386:         msk = torch.concat([torch.repeat_interleave(msk[:, 0:1], repeats=4, dim=1), msk[:, 1:]], dim=1)
00387:         msk = msk.view(1, msk.shape[1] // 4, 4, height // 8, width // 8)
00388:         msk = msk.transpose(1, 2)[0]
00389: 
00390:         y = vae.encode([vae_input.to(dtype=torch_dtype, device=device)], device=device,
00391:                        tiled=True, tile_size=(30, 52), tile_stride=(15, 26))[0]
00392:         y = y.to(dtype=torch_dtype, device=device)
00393:         y = torch.concat([msk, y])
00394:         y = y.unsqueeze(0)
00395:         y = y.to(dtype=torch_dtype, device=device)
00396:         return {"y": y}
00397: 
00398: 
00399: # =============================================================================
00400: # Scheduler
00401: # =============================================================================
00402:

00058: def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
00059:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
00060:     with path.open("rb") as file:
00061:         for chunk in iter(lambda: file.read(4 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
00062:             digest.update(chunk)
00063:     return digest.hexdigest()

00106:             previous_valid = initial_valid
00107:         else:
00108:             previous_uv = current_uv[frame_index - 1]
00109:             previous_valid = current_valid[frame_index - 1]
00110: 
00111:         expected = current_uv[frame_index] - previous_uv
00112:         x = previous_uv[:, 0].round().to(torch.long).clamp(0, 511)
00113:         y = previous_uv[:, 1].round().to(torch.long).clamp(0, 511)
00114:         flow_tensor = torch.from_numpy(flows[frame_index])
00115:         sampled = flow_tensor[:, y, x].T
00116:         sampled_nonzero = torch.linalg.vector_norm(sampled, dim=1) > 1e-4
00117:         valid = previous_valid & current_valid[frame_index] & sampled_nonzero
00118:         visibility_filter_applied = flow_source_point_indices is not None

00124:                 sampled_source < object_point_end

00134: def _repeatability(output_a: Path, output_b: Path) -> dict:
00135:     export_a = torch.load(output_a / "point_trajectories.pt", map_location="cpu")
00136:     export_b = torch.load(output_b / "point_trajectories.pt", map_location="cpu")
00137:     points_a = export_a["objects"][0]["points_3d"]
00138:     points_b = export_b["objects"][0]["points_3d"]
00139:     uv_a = export_a["objects"][0]["points_uv"]
00140:     uv_b = export_b["objects"][0]["points_uv"]
00141:     flows_a = np.load(output_a / "flows.npy")
00142:     flows_b = np.load(output_b / "flows.npy")
00143:     point_error = torch.linalg.vector_norm(points_a - points_b, dim=-1).numpy()
00144:     uv_error = torch.linalg.vector_norm(uv_a - uv_b, dim=-1).numpy()
00145:     flow_error = np.linalg.norm(flows_a - flows_b, axis=1)
00146:     support_a = np.linalg.norm(flows_a, axis=1) > 1e-4
00147:     support_b = np.linalg.norm(flows_b, axis=1) > 1e-4
00148:     shared_support = support_a & support_b
00149:     union_support = support_a | support_b
00150:     images_a = sorted(output_a.glob("frame_*.png"))
00151:     images_b = sorted(output_b.glob("frame_*.png"))
00152:     image_hashes_a = [_sha256(path) for path in images_a]
00153:     image_hashes_b = [_sha256(path) for path in images_b]

00059: def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
00060:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
00061:     with path.open("rb") as file:
00062:         for chunk in iter(lambda: file.read(4 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
00063:             digest.update(chunk)
00064:     return digest.hexdigest()

00586:     def save_optical_flow(self, optical_flow, valid_mask, frame_id):
00587: 
00588:         # Extract flow components
00589:         flow_x = optical_flow[:, :, 0].cpu().numpy()
00590:         flow_y = optical_flow[:, :, 1].cpu().numpy()
00591:         valid_mask_np = valid_mask.cpu().numpy()
00592:         
00593:         # Convert flow to HSV color representation
00594:         angle = np.arctan2(-flow_y, flow_x)
00595:         
00596:         # Create HSV image
00597:         hsv = np.zeros((optical_flow.shape[0], optical_flow.shape[1], 3), dtype=np.uint8)
00598:         hsv[..., 0] = (angle + np.pi) / (2 * np.pi) * 179
00599:         hsv[..., 1] = 255
00600:         hsv[..., 2] = 255
00601:         # magnitude = np.sqrt(flow_x**2 + flow_y**2)
00602:         # hsv[..., 2] = np.clip(magnitude * 255 / np.max(magnitude), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
00603:         
00604:         # Apply valid mask
00605:         hsv[~valid_mask_np] = 0
00606:         
00607:         # Convert HSV to RGB
00608:         flow_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(hsv, cv2.COLOR_HSV2RGB)

00648:     def _compute_optical_flow_pytorch3d_style(self, current_fg_points, prev_fg_points, 
00649:                                         current_camera, prev_camera, image_size=512, frame_id=0):
00650:         
00651:         if current_fg_points.shape[0] > prev_fg_points.shape[0]:
00652:             current_fg_points = current_fg_points[:prev_fg_points.shape[0]]
00653:         elif prev_fg_points.shape[0] > current_fg_points.shape[0]:
00654:             prev_more = prev_fg_points[-(prev_fg_points.shape[0] - current_fg_points.shape[0]):]
00655:             current_fg_points = torch.cat([current_fg_points, prev_more], dim=0)
00656:         
00657:         current_uv = self._proj_uv(current_fg_points, current_camera, image_size)
00658:         prev_uv = self._proj_uv(prev_fg_points, prev_camera, image_size)
00659:         
00660:         delta_uv = current_uv - prev_uv

00011: class CausalInferencePipeline(torch.nn.Module):
00012:     """Pipeline for causal video generation inference."""
00013: 
00014:     def __init__(self, args, device, generator=None, text_encoder=None, vae=None):
00015:         super().__init__()
00016: 
00017:         if args.i2v_flow:
00018:             self.i2v_flow = True
00019:             Wrapper = WanI2VDiffusionWrapper
00020:         else:
00021:             self.i2v_flow = False
00022:             Wrapper = WanDiffusionWrapper
00023: 
00024:         # Initialize models
00025:         self.generator = Wrapper(
00026:             **getattr(args, "model_kwargs", {}), is_causal=True) if generator is None else generator
00027:         self.text_encoder = WanTextEncoder() if text_encoder is None else text_encoder
00028:         self.vae = WanVAEWrapper() if vae is None else vae
00029: 
00030:         # Initialize causal hyperparameters
00031:         self.scheduler = self.generator.get_scheduler()
00032:         self.denoising_step_list = torch.tensor(args.denoising_step_list, dtype=torch.long)
00033:         if args.warp_denoising_step:
00034:             timesteps = torch.cat((self.scheduler.timesteps.cpu(), torch.tensor([0], dtype=torch.float32)))
00035:             self.denoising_step_list = timesteps[1000 - self.denoising_step_list]
00036:

00038:         self.frame_seq_length = 1560

00049: 
00050: class CausalInferencePipelineSDEdit(torch.nn.Module):
00051:     """Pipeline for causal video generation inference with SDEdit support."""
00052: 
00053:     def __init__(self, args, device, generator=None, text_encoder=None, vae=None,
00054:                  use_separate_encode_vae=False):
00055:         super().__init__()
00056: 
00057:         if args.i2v_flow:
00058:             self.i2v_flow = True
00059:             Wrapper = WanI2VDiffusionWrapper
00060:         else:
00061:             self.i2v_flow = False
00062:             Wrapper = WanDiffusionWrapper
00063: 
00064:         # Initialize models
00065:         self.generator = Wrapper(
00066:             **getattr(args, "model_kwargs", {}), is_causal=True) if generator is None else generator
00067:         self.text_encoder = WanTextEncoder() if text_encoder is None else text_encoder
00068:         self.vae = WanVAEWrapper() if vae is None else vae
00069: 
00070:         # Separate VAE for encoding sim_latents to avoid cache conflicts
00071:         # with the decoder's streaming cache on self.vae
00072:         if use_separate_encode_vae:
00073:             self.encode_vae = WanVAEWrapper()
00074:         else:
00075:             self.encode_vae = self.vae
00076: 
00077:         # Initialize causal hyperparameters
00078:         self.scheduler = self.generator.get_scheduler()
00079:         self.denoising_step_list = torch.tensor(args.denoising_step_list, dtype=torch.long)
00080: 
00081:         # Detect SDEdit mode: if the first denoising step < total scheduler steps,
00082:         # we start from a noised version of sim_latent instead of pure noise.
00083:         if self.denoising_step_list[0] < len(self.scheduler.timesteps):
00084:             self.sdedit = True
00085:         else:
00086:             self.sdedit = False

00371: class WanVideoUnit_ImageEmbedderVAE(PipelineUnit):
00372:     """VAE image embedder for WAN video pipeline."""
00373: 
00374:     def __init__(self):
00375:         super().__init__(
00376:             input_params=("input_image", "end_image", "num_frames", "height", "width"),
00377:             onload_model_names=("vae",)
00378:         )
00379: 
00380:     def process(self, vae, input_image, end_image, num_frames, height, width, device, torch_dtype):
00381:         image = input_image.to(device)
00382:         msk = torch.ones(1, num_frames, height // 8, width // 8, device=device)
00383:         msk[:, 1:] = 0
00384:         vae_input = torch.concat([image.transpose(0, 1),
00385:                                   torch.zeros(3, num_frames - 1, height, width).to(image.device)], dim=1)
00386:         msk = torch.concat([torch.repeat_interleave(msk[:, 0:1], repeats=4, dim=1), msk[:, 1:]], dim=1)
00387:         msk = msk.view(1, msk.shape[1] // 4, 4, height // 8, width // 8)
00388:         msk = msk.transpose(1, 2)[0]
00389: 
00390:         y = vae.encode([vae_input.to(dtype=torch_dtype, device=device)], device=device,
00391:                        tiled=True, tile_size=(30, 52), tile_stride=(15, 26))[0]
00392:         y = y.to(dtype=torch_dtype, device=device)
00393:         y = torch.concat([msk, y])
00394:         y = y.unsqueeze(0)
00395:         y = y.to(dtype=torch_dtype, device=device)
00396:         return {"y": y}
